(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"70c57b0d-8dbd-49ea-ac0e-5bfe1df64e0f","uuid":"f70b0f0a-aa0d-4754-9d84-b82d9bd72ada","timestamp":"2026-07-27T11:25:35.484Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-07-27T111530Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/server.ts","oldString":"import { createHash, randomUUID } from \"node:crypto\";","newString":"import { randomUUID } from \"node:crypto\";","originalFile":"/**\n * forecast.example.com — service side.\n *\n * Sells point weather forecasts to autonomous agents for $0.35/call, settled in\n * USDC on Base. See design.md.\n *\n *   discovery : ERC-8004 Identity Registry (onchain) -> tokenURI -> agent card\n *   payment   : x402 (HTTP 402 + X-PAYMENT) over EIP-3009 transferWithAuthorization\n *   gasless   : the facilitator broadcasts and pays gas; the caller only signs\n *   ratings   : ERC-8004 Reputation Registry, gated by a receipt we sign per call\n *\n * No accounts, no API keys, no human in any step.\n *\n * Run: npx tsx server.ts     (Node >= 20 for global fetch)\n */\n\nimport { createServer, IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from \"node:http\";\nimport { createHash, randomUUID } from \"node:crypto\";\nimport {\n  createPublicClient,\n  http as viemHttp,\n  getAddress,\n  isAddress,\n  keccak256,\n  toHex,\n  type Address,\n  type Hex,\n} from \"viem\";\nimport { base } from \"viem/chains\";\nimport { privateKeyToAccount } from \"viem/accounts\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Config. Everything chain-shaped is config, not a literal buried in logic, and\n// is re-verified against the chain at boot (see verifyChainConfig).\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst env = (k: string, fallback?: string): string => {\n  const v = process.env[k] ?? fallback;\n  if (v === undefined) throw new Error(`missing required env ${k}`);\n  return v;\n};\n\nconst CONFIG = {\n  port: Number(env(\"PORT\", \"8080\")),\n  origin: env(\"ORIGIN\", \"https://forecast.example.com\"),\n\n  // --- payment ---\n  network: \"base\" as const,\n  chainId: 8453,\n  /** Native USDC on Base. Implements EIP-3009. Bridged USDbC does NOT — do not swap this. */\n  usdc: getAddress(env(\"USDC_ADDRESS\", \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\")),\n  /** $0.35 in USDC atomic units (6 decimals). */\n  priceAtomic: BigInt(env(\"PRICE_ATOMIC\", \"350000\")),\n  /** Where the USDC lands. Cold key, separate from the signer. */\n  treasury: getAddress(env(\"TREASURY_ADDRESS\", \"0x000000000000000000000000000000000000dEaD\")),\n  facilitatorUrl: env(\"FACILITATOR_URL\", \"https://x402.org/facilitator\").replace(/\\/$/, \"\"),\n  facilitatorAuth: process.env.FACILITATOR_API_KEY, // optional, provider-specific\n\n  // --- identity / reputation (ERC-8004) ---\n  agentId: BigInt(env(\"AGENT_ID\", \"4711\")),\n  identityRegistry: getAddress(\n    env(\"IDENTITY_REGISTRY\", \"0x000000000000000000000000000000000000dEaD\"),\n  ),\n  reputationRegistry: getAddress(\n    env(\"REPUTATION_REGISTRY\", \"0x000000000000000000000000000000000000dEaD\"),\n  ),\n  validationRegistry: process.env.VALIDATION_REGISTRY,\n\n  rpcUrl: env(\"BASE_RPC_URL\", \"https://mainnet.base.org\"),\n  /** Hot key in KMS in production. Signs receipts + response attestations. Holds no funds. */\n  operatorKey: env(\"AGENT_OPERATOR_KEY\", `0x${\"11\".repeat(32)}`) as Hex,\n\n  upstreamUrl: env(\"WEATHER_UPSTREAM\", \"https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast\"),\n\n  /** Reject payments whose authorization expires too soon to survive settlement. */\n  minValiditySecondsLeft: 30,\n  maxTimeoutSeconds: 60,\n  /** A receipt is usable for one rating, for this long. */\n  receiptTtlSeconds: 30 * 24 * 3600,\n} as const;\n\nconst operator = privateKeyToAccount(CONFIG.operatorKey);\nconst rpc = createPublicClient({ chain: base, transport: viemHttp(CONFIG.rpcUrl) });\n\n/**\n * USDC's EIP-712 domain, read from chain at boot. Clients need name+version to\n * produce a valid EIP-3009 signature; a hardcoded guess that drifts produces\n * signatures that fail onchain with no useful error message.\n */\nlet usdcDomain = { name: \"USD Coin\", version: \"2\" };\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// x402 types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface PaymentRequirements {\n  scheme: \"exact\";\n  network: string;\n  maxAmountRequired: string;\n  resource: string;\n  description: string;\n  mimeType: string;\n  payTo: Address;\n  maxTimeoutSeconds: number;\n  asset: Address;\n  outputSchema?: unknown;\n  extra: { name: string; version: string };\n}\n\ninterface ExactEvmAuthorization {\n  from: Address;\n  to: Address;\n  value: string;\n  validAfter: string;\n  validBefore: string;\n  nonce: Hex;\n}\n\ninterface PaymentPayload {\n  x402Version: number;\n  scheme: string;\n  network: string;\n  payload: { signature: Hex; authorization: ExactEvmAuthorization };\n}\n\ninterface SettleResult {\n  success: boolean;\n  errorReason?: string;\n  transaction?: Hex;\n  network?: string;\n  payer?: Address;\n}\n\nconst X402_VERSION = 1;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Facilitator. Single seam: swap this for a self-hosted relayer that broadcasts\n// transferWithAuthorization itself without touching anything else.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface Facilitator {\n  verify(\n    p: PaymentPayload,\n    r: PaymentRequirements,\n  ): Promise<{ isValid: boolean; invalidReason?: string; payer?: Address }>;\n  settle(p: PaymentPayload, r: PaymentRequirements): Promise<SettleResult>;\n}\n\nconst httpFacilitator: Facilitator = {\n  async verify(paymentPayload, paymentRequirements) {\n    return post(\"/verify\", { x402Version: X402_VERSION, paymentPayload, paymentRequirements });\n  },\n  async settle(paymentPayload, paymentRequirements) {\n    return post(\"/settle\", { x402Version: X402_VERSION, paymentPayload, paymentRequirements });\n  },\n};\n\nasync function post(path: string, body: unknown): Promise<any> {\n  const headers: Record<string, string> = { \"content-type\": \"application/json\" };\n  if (CONFIG.facilitatorAuth) headers.authorization = `Bearer ${CONFIG.facilitatorAuth}`;\n\n  const res = await fetch(CONFIG.facilitatorUrl + path, {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers,\n    body: JSON.stringify(body, jsonBigint),\n    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(CONFIG.maxTimeoutSeconds * 1000),\n  });\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    throw new Error(`facilitator ${path} -> ${res.status} ${await res.text().catch(() => \"\")}`);\n  }\n  return res.json();\n}\n\nconst jsonBigint = (_k: string, v: unknown) => (typeof v === \"bigint\" ? v.toString() : v);\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Idempotency. Keyed on the EIP-3009 nonce, which USDC itself consumes on\n// settle. Without this, a client whose connection drops after settlement has\n// paid and cannot retry: the replayed authorization now reverts onchain.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface Settled {\n  nonce: Hex;\n  payer: Address;\n  txHash?: Hex;\n  body: unknown;\n  receipt: unknown;\n  at: number;\n}\n\nconst settledCalls = new Map<Hex, Settled>();\nconst SETTLED_TTL_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;\n\nsetInterval(() => {\n  const cutoff = Date.now() - SETTLED_TTL_MS;\n  for (const [k, v] of settledCalls) if (v.at < cutoff) settledCalls.delete(k);\n}, 60_000).unref();\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Published documents\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst caip10 = (addr: Address) => `eip155:${CONFIG.chainId}:${addr}`;\n\nfunction paymentRequirements(resource: string, description: string): PaymentRequirements {\n  return {\n    scheme: \"exact\",\n    network: CONFIG.network,\n    maxAmountRequired: CONFIG.priceAtomic.toString(),\n    resource,\n    description,\n    mimeType: \"application/json\",\n    payTo: CONFIG.treasury,\n    maxTimeoutSeconds: CONFIG.maxTimeoutSeconds,\n    asset: CONFIG.usdc,\n    outputSchema: FORECAST_OUTPUT_SCHEMA,\n    extra: { name: usdcDomain.name, version: usdcDomain.version },\n  };\n}\n\nconst FORECAST_OUTPUT_SCHEMA = {\n  type: \"object\",\n  required: [\"location\", \"generatedAt\", \"hourly\"],\n  properties: {\n    location: { type: \"object\", properties: { lat: { type: \"number\" }, lon: { type: \"number\" } } },\n    generatedAt: { type: \"string\", format: \"date-time\" },\n    units: { type: \"object\" },\n    hourly: { type: \"array\", items: { type: \"object\" } },\n  },\n} as const;\n\n/**\n * A2A Agent Card extended with the ERC-8004 fields. Served at\n * /.well-known/agent-card.json, which is what the identity NFT's tokenURI\n * points at. `registrations[].signature` closes the loop offchain -> onchain so\n * a stranger can verify both directions before trusting anything here.\n */\nfunction agentCard() {\n  return {\n    protocolVersion: \"0.3.0\",\n    name: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description:\n      \"Point weather forecasts, 0-168h, global. $0.35 per call, USDC on Base, x402. No account required.\",\n    url: `${CONFIG.origin}/v1`,\n    version: \"1.2.0\",\n    capabilities: { streaming: false, pushNotifications: false, stateTransitionHistory: false },\n    defaultInputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    defaultOutputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n\n    skills: [\n      {\n        id: \"point-forecast\",\n        name: \"Point forecast\",\n        description: \"Hourly and daily forecast for a lat/lon, up to 168 hours ahead.\",\n        tags: [\"weather\", \"forecast\", \"x402\", \"usdc\"],\n        inputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n        outputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n        examples: [`GET ${CONFIG.origin}/v1/forecast?lat=52.52&lon=13.40&hours=48`],\n      },\n    ],\n\n    // ERC-8004: binds this document back to the onchain identity.\n    registrations: [\n      {\n        agentId: Number(CONFIG.agentId),\n        agentRegistry: caip10(CONFIG.identityRegistry),\n        signature: process.env.REGISTRATION_SIGNATURE ?? \"0x\",\n      },\n    ],\n    trustModels: [\"reputation\", \"crypto-economic\"],\n\n    // How to pay. The 402 challenge is authoritative; this is a hint for planners.\n    x402: {\n      x402Version: X402_VERSION,\n      endpoints: [\n        {\n          resource: `${CONFIG.origin}/v1/forecast`,\n          method: \"GET\",\n          ...paymentRequirements(`${CONFIG.origin}/v1/forecast`, \"Point weather forecast\"),\n        },\n      ],\n    },\n\n    // Where ratings go, and which key signs the receipts that authorize them.\n    feedback: {\n      registry: caip10(CONFIG.reputationRegistry),\n      authScheme: \"erc8004-feedback-auth-v1\",\n      signer: operator.address,\n      relay: `${CONFIG.origin}/v1/feedback/relay`,\n      supportedTags: [\"accuracy\", \"latency\", \"availability\"],\n    },\n    validation: CONFIG.validationRegistry\n      ? { registry: caip10(getAddress(CONFIG.validationRegistry)) }\n      : undefined,\n\n    openapi: `${CONFIG.origin}/openapi.json`,\n  };\n}\n\n/** x402 resource list — the machine-readable price sheet. */\nfunction x402Directory() {\n  return {\n    x402Version: X402_VERSION,\n    resources: [\n      {\n        resource: `${CONFIG.origin}/v1/forecast`,\n        method: \"GET\",\n        accepts: [paymentRequirements(`${CONFIG.origin}/v1/forecast`, \"Point weather forecast\")],\n        lastUpdated: new Date().toISOString(),\n      },\n    ],\n  };\n}\n\nfunction openapi() {\n  return {\n    openapi: \"3.1.0\",\n    info: {\n      title: \"forecast.example.com\",\n      version: \"1.2.0\",\n      description: \"Weather forecasts priced per call in USDC on Base via x402.\",\n    },\n    servers: [{ url: `${CONFIG.origin}/v1` }],\n    paths: {\n      \"/forecast\": {\n        get: {\n          summary: \"Point forecast\",\n          parameters: [\n            { name: \"lat\", in: \"query\", required: true, schema: { type: \"number\" } },\n            { name: \"lon\", in: \"query\", required: true, schema: { type: \"number\" } },\n            {\n              name: \"hours\",\n              in: \"query\",\n              schema: { type: \"integer\", minimum: 1, maximum: 168, default: 48 },\n            },\n            {\n              name: \"X-PAYMENT\",\n              in: \"header\",\n              required: true,\n              schema: { type: \"string\" },\n              description: \"base64 x402 payment payload (EIP-3009 authorization + signature)\",\n            },\n          ],\n          responses: {\n            \"200\": { description: \"Forecast + signed feedback receipt\" },\n            \"402\": { description: \"Payment required; body carries x402 `accepts`\" },\n            \"502\": { description: \"Upstream unavailable; no payment was settled\" },\n          },\n        },\n      },\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Payment validation\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction decodePaymentHeader(header: string): PaymentPayload {\n  const json = Buffer.from(header, \"base64\").toString(\"utf8\");\n  const parsed = JSON.parse(json) as PaymentPayload;\n  if (!parsed?.payload?.authorization || !parsed.payload.signature) {\n    throw new Error(\"malformed payment payload\");\n  }\n  return parsed;\n}\n\n/**\n * Local structural checks, run BEFORE the facilitator call. Never trust the\n * payload's own claims about what it is paying for: an attacker-supplied\n * payload should cost us a few microseconds, not a facilitator round trip.\n */\nfunction checkRequirements(p: PaymentPayload, req: PaymentRequirements): string | null {\n  const a = p.payload.authorization;\n  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);\n\n  if (p.x402Version !== X402_VERSION) return `unsupported x402Version ${p.x402Version}`;\n  if (p.scheme !== req.scheme) return `unsupported scheme ${p.scheme}`;\n  if (p.network !== req.network) return `wrong network ${p.network}, expected ${req.network}`;\n  if (!isAddress(a.from) || !isAddress(a.to)) return \"bad address in authorization\";\n  if (getAddress(a.to) !== req.payTo) return \"authorization pays the wrong recipient\";\n\n  let value: bigint;\n  try {\n    value = BigInt(a.value);\n  } catch {\n    return \"bad value\";\n  }\n  if (value < BigInt(req.maxAmountRequired)) {\n    return `underpaid: ${a.value} < ${req.maxAmountRequired}`;\n  }\n\n  if (!/^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/.test(a.nonce)) return \"nonce must be 32 bytes\";\n  if (Number(a.validAfter) > now) return \"authorization not yet valid\";\n  if (Number(a.validBefore) < now + CONFIG.minValiditySecondsLeft) {\n    return \"authorization expires too soon to settle\";\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Feedback receipt (ERC-8004). Signed only after settlement, before we know the\n// score, and usable against a registry we do not control — so we cannot\n// suppress a bad rating, and a forged one costs a real $0.35 from a real payer.\n//\n// NOTE: ERC-8004 is a draft. Pin this struct + domain to the ABI of the\n// registry you actually mint into before going live.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst FEEDBACK_AUTH_TYPES = {\n  FeedbackAuth: [\n    { name: \"agentId\", type: \"uint256\" },\n    { name: \"clientAddress\", type: \"address\" },\n    { name: \"indexLimit\", type: \"uint256\" },\n    { name: \"expiry\", type: \"uint256\" },\n    { name: \"chainId\", type: \"uint256\" },\n    { name: \"identityRegistry\", type: \"address\" },\n    { name: \"signerAddress\", type: \"address\" },\n  ],\n} as const;\n\nasync function issueFeedbackReceipt(payer: Address, settlementTx?: Hex) {\n  const domain = {\n    name: \"ERC8004ReputationRegistry\",\n    version: \"1\",\n    chainId: CONFIG.chainId,\n    verifyingContract: CONFIG.reputationRegistry,\n  } as const;\n\n  const message = {\n    agentId: CONFIG.agentId,\n    clientAddress: payer,\n    indexLimit: 1n, // one rating per paid call\n    expiry: BigInt(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + CONFIG.receiptTtlSeconds),\n    chainId: BigInt(CONFIG.chainId),\n    identityRegistry: CONFIG.identityRegistry,\n    signerAddress: operator.address,\n  } as const;\n\n  const signature = await operator.signTypedData({\n    domain,\n    types: FEEDBACK_AUTH_TYPES,\n    primaryType: \"FeedbackAuth\",\n    message,\n  });\n\n  return {\n    scheme: \"erc8004-feedback-auth-v1\",\n    domain,\n    primaryType: \"FeedbackAuth\",\n    message: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(message, jsonBigint)),\n    signature,\n    settlementTx,\n    howTo: {\n      contract: caip10(CONFIG.reputationRegistry),\n      method:\n        \"giveFeedback(uint256 agentId, uint8 score, bytes32 tag1, bytes32 tag2, string fileuri, bytes32 filehash, bytes feedbackAuth)\",\n      scoreRange: [0, 100],\n      tags: [\"accuracy\", \"latency\", \"availability\"],\n      gaslessRelay: `${CONFIG.origin}/v1/feedback/relay`,\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n/** Attributable response: lets a client cite a bad answer as evidence later. */\nasync function attestResponse(requestHash: Hex, body: unknown): Promise<Hex> {\n  const responseHash = keccak256(toHex(JSON.stringify(body)));\n  return operator.signMessage({\n    message: { raw: keccak256(toHex(`${CONFIG.agentId}:${requestHash}:${responseHash}`)) },\n  });\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// The product\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface ForecastQuery {\n  lat: number;\n  lon: number;\n  hours: number;\n  units: \"metric\" | \"imperial\";\n}\n\nfunction parseForecastQuery(url: URL): ForecastQuery | { error: string } {\n  const lat = Number(url.searchParams.get(\"lat\"));\n  const lon = Number(url.searchParams.get(\"lon\"));\n  const hours = Number(url.searchParams.get(\"hours\") ?? \"48\");\n  const units = (url.searchParams.get(\"units\") ?? \"metric\") as ForecastQuery[\"units\"];\n\n  if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || lat < -90 || lat > 90) return { error: \"lat must be -90..90\" };\n  if (!Number.isFinite(lon) || lon < -180 || lon > 180) return { error: \"lon must be -180..180\" };\n  if (!Number.isInteger(hours) || hours < 1 || hours > 168) return { error: \"hours must be 1..168\" };\n  if (units !== \"metric\" && units !== \"imperial\") return { error: \"units must be metric|imperial\" };\n  return { lat, lon, hours, units };\n}\n\nasync function fetchForecast(q: ForecastQuery) {\n  const u = new URL(CONFIG.upstreamUrl);\n  u.searchParams.set(\"latitude\", String(q.lat));\n  u.searchParams.set(\"longitude\", String(q.lon));\n  u.searchParams.set(\"hourly\", \"temperature_2m,precipitation,wind_speed_10m,weather_code\");\n  u.searchParams.set(\"forecast_hours\", String(q.hours));\n  if (q.units === \"imperial\") {\n    u.searchParams.set(\"temperature_unit\", \"fahrenheit\");\n    u.searchParams.set(\"wind_speed_unit\", \"mph\");\n  }\n\n  const res = await fetch(u, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000) });\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`upstream ${res.status}`);\n  const raw = (await res.json()) as any;\n\n  const times: string[] = raw.hourly?.time ?? [];\n  return {\n    location: { lat: q.lat, lon: q.lon },\n    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    units: {\n      temperature: q.units === \"imperial\" ? \"degF\" : \"degC\",\n      windSpeed: q.units === \"imperial\" ? \"mph\" : \"km/h\",\n      precipitation: \"mm\",\n    },\n    hourly: times.slice(0, q.hours).map((t, i) => ({\n      time: t,\n      temperature: raw.hourly.temperature_2m?.[i] ?? null,\n      precipitation: raw.hourly.precipitation?.[i] ?? null,\n      windSpeed: raw.hourly.wind_speed_10m?.[i] ?? null,\n      weatherCode: raw.hourly.weather_code?.[i] ?? null,\n    })),\n    source: \"open-meteo\",\n  };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Handlers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction send(res: ServerResponse, status: number, body: unknown, headers: Record<string, string> = {}) {\n  const payload = typeof body === \"string\" ? body : JSON.stringify(body, jsonBigint, 2);\n  res.writeHead(status, {\n    \"content-type\": \"application/json; charset=utf-8\",\n    \"access-control-allow-origin\": \"*\",\n    \"access-control-expose-headers\": \"x-payment-response, x-agent-signature\",\n    ...headers,\n  });\n  res.end(payload);\n}\n\nfunction challenge(res: ServerResponse, resource: string, error: string, status = 402) {\n  send(res, status, {\n    x402Version: X402_VERSION,\n    error,\n    accepts: [paymentRequirements(resource, \"Point weather forecast, 0-168h\")],\n  });\n}\n\n/** The paid endpoint. Ordering — verify, work, settle, respond — is deliberate. */\nasync function handleForecast(req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse, url: URL) {\n  const resource = `${CONFIG.origin}${url.pathname}${url.search}`;\n\n  const q = parseForecastQuery(url);\n  if (\"error\" in q) return send(res, 400, { error: q.error });\n\n  const header = req.headers[\"x-payment\"];\n  if (!header || typeof header !== \"string\") {\n    return challenge(res, resource, \"X-PAYMENT header is required\");\n  }\n\n  let payment: PaymentPayload;\n  try {\n    payment = decodePaymentHeader(header);\n  } catch (e) {\n    return challenge(res, resource, `invalid X-PAYMENT header: ${(e as Error).message}`);\n  }\n\n  const nonce = payment.payload.authorization.nonce;\n\n  // Idempotent retry of an already-settled call: they paid, give them the data.\n  const prior = settledCalls.get(nonce);\n  if (prior) {\n    return send(res, 200, { ...(prior.body as object), receipt: prior.receipt, replayed: true }, {\n      \"x-payment-response\": encodeSettleHeader({\n        success: true,\n        transaction: prior.txHash,\n        network: CONFIG.network,\n        payer: prior.payer,\n      }),\n    });\n  }\n\n  const requirements = paymentRequirements(resource, \"Point weather forecast, 0-168h\");\n\n  // 1. Local checks, then facilitator verification. No work done yet.\n  const structural = checkRequirements(payment, requirements);\n  if (structural) return challenge(res, resource, structural);\n\n  let verification: { isValid: boolean; invalidReason?: string; payer?: Address };\n  try {\n    verification = await httpFacilitator.verify(payment, requirements);\n  } catch (e) {\n    return send(res, 503, { error: `payment verification unavailable: ${(e as Error).message}` });\n  }\n  if (!verification.isValid) {\n    return challenge(res, resource, verification.invalidReason ?? \"payment verification failed\");\n  }\n\n  // 2. Do the work before settling. If the upstream is down the caller is never\n  //    charged: the authorization simply expires unused. We hold no balances,\n  //    so there is nothing to refund.\n  let forecast: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof fetchForecast>>;\n  try {\n    forecast = await fetchForecast(q);\n  } catch (e) {\n    return send(res, 502, {\n      error: \"upstream forecast source unavailable\",\n      detail: (e as Error).message,\n      charged: false,\n    });\n  }\n\n  // 3. Settle before responding, so data never leaves for a payment that failed\n  //    to land onchain. The facilitator submits transferWithAuthorization and\n  //    pays the gas — this is why the caller needs no ETH.\n  let settlement: SettleResult;\n  try {\n    settlement = await httpFacilitator.settle(payment, requirements);\n  } catch (e) {\n    return challenge(res, resource, `settlement failed: ${(e as Error).message}`);\n  }\n  if (!settlement.success) {\n    return challenge(res, resource, settlement.errorReason ?? \"settlement failed\");\n  }\n\n  // 4. Respond with the data, a signed attestation, and a receipt that lets the\n  //    caller rate us onchain without asking us for anything further.\n  const payer = getAddress(settlement.payer ?? verification.payer ?? payment.payload.authorization.from);\n  const requestHash = keccak256(toHex(resource));\n  const receipt = await issueFeedbackReceipt(payer, settlement.transaction);\n  const body = { ...forecast, requestId: randomUUID(), agentId: Number(CONFIG.agentId) };\n\n  settledCalls.set(nonce, {\n    nonce,\n    payer,\n    txHash: settlement.transaction,\n    body,\n    receipt,\n    at: Date.now(),\n  });\n\n  send(res, 200, { ...body, receipt }, {\n    \"x-payment-response\": encodeSettleHeader(settlement),\n    \"x-agent-signature\": await attestResponse(requestHash, body),\n  });\n}\n\nconst encodeSettleHeader = (s: SettleResult) =>\n  Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(s, jsonBigint)).toString(\"base64\");\n\n/**\n * Gasless rating relay for callers holding USDC but no ETH: they sign the\n * feedback intent, we pay the gas to submit it. The score lives inside their\n * signature, so we cannot alter it — and withholding negative ones is visible,\n * since settlement txs and feedback events are both public.\n */\nasync function handleFeedbackRelay(req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse) {\n  let intent: any;\n  try {\n    intent = JSON.parse(await readBody(req));\n  } catch {\n    return send(res, 400, { error: \"body must be JSON\" });\n  }\n\n  const required = [\"score\", \"signature\", \"feedbackAuth\", \"clientAddress\"];\n  const missing = required.filter((f) => intent[f] === undefined);\n  if (missing.length) return send(res, 400, { error: `missing fields: ${missing.join(\", \")}` });\n  if (!Number.isInteger(intent.score) || intent.score < 0 || intent.score > 100) {\n    return send(res, 400, { error: \"score must be an integer 0..100\" });\n  }\n\n  // Submitting requires a gas-funded relayer wallet + nonce management; wire the\n  // walletClient here once the registry's signed-intent entrypoint is pinned.\n  // Until then, tell the caller exactly how to submit it themselves.\n  return send(res, 501, {\n    error: \"relay not enabled on this deployment\",\n    submitYourself: {\n      contract: caip10(CONFIG.reputationRegistry),\n      method: \"giveFeedback\",\n      note: \"Or use any public ERC-4337 paymaster that accepts USDC for gas.\",\n    },\n  });\n}\n\nasync function handleHealth(res: ServerResponse) {\n  const [upstream, facilitator, chain] = await Promise.all([\n    probe(`${CONFIG.upstreamUrl}?latitude=0&longitude=0&hourly=temperature_2m&forecast_hours=1`),\n    probe(`${CONFIG.facilitatorUrl}/supported`),\n    rpc.getBlockNumber().then(\n      (n) => ({ ok: true, detail: `block ${n}` }),\n      (e) => ({ ok: false, detail: (e as Error).message }),\n    ),\n  ]);\n  const ok = upstream.ok && chain.ok;\n  send(res, ok ? 200 : 503, {\n    ok,\n    agentId: Number(CONFIG.agentId),\n    checks: { upstream, facilitator, chain },\n  });\n}\n\nasync function probe(url: string) {\n  try {\n    const r = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(4000) });\n    return { ok: r.ok, detail: `HTTP ${r.status}` };\n  } catch (e) {\n    return { ok: false, detail: (e as Error).message };\n  }\n}\n\nfunction readBody(req: IncomingMessage): Promise<string> {\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    let data = \"\";\n    req.on(\"data\", (c) => {\n      data += c;\n      if (data.length > 64_000) reject(new Error(\"body too large\"));\n    });\n    req.on(\"end\", () => resolve(data));\n    req.on(\"error\", reject);\n  });\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Router\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst server = createServer(async (req, res) => {\n  const url = new URL(req.url ?? \"/\", CONFIG.origin);\n\n  if (req.method === \"OPTIONS\") {\n    res.writeHead(204, {\n      \"access-control-allow-origin\": \"*\",\n      \"access-control-allow-headers\": \"content-type, x-payment\",\n      \"access-control-allow-methods\": \"GET, POST, OPTIONS\",\n    });\n    return res.end();\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const route = `${req.method} ${url.pathname}`;\n    switch (route) {\n      // --- discovery: free, static, cacheable, no payment ---\n      case \"GET /.well-known/agent-card.json\":\n        return send(res, 200, agentCard(), { \"cache-control\": \"public, max-age=300\" });\n      case \"GET /.well-known/x402\":\n        return send(res, 200, x402Directory(), { \"cache-control\": \"public, max-age=300\" });\n      case \"GET /openapi.json\":\n        return send(res, 200, openapi(), { \"cache-control\": \"public, max-age=300\" });\n\n      // --- the 402 challenge on its own, for planning ---\n      case \"GET /v1/quote\":\n        return challenge(\n          res,\n          `${CONFIG.origin}/v1/forecast${url.search}`,\n          \"quote only; resend to /v1/forecast with X-PAYMENT\",\n          200,\n        );\n\n      // --- the product ---\n      case \"GET /v1/forecast\":\n        return await handleForecast(req, res, url);\n\n      // --- ratings ---\n      case \"POST /v1/feedback/relay\":\n        return await handleFeedbackRelay(req, res);\n\n      case \"GET /healthz\":\n        return await handleHealth(res);\n\n      default: {\n        if (req.method === \"GET\" && url.pathname.startsWith(\"/v1/receipt/\")) {\n          const nonce = url.pathname.slice(\"/v1/receipt/\".length) as Hex;\n          const prior = settledCalls.get(nonce);\n          if (!prior) return send(res, 404, { error: \"unknown or expired settlement nonce\" });\n          return send(res, 200, { receipt: prior.receipt, settlementTx: prior.txHash });\n        }\n        return send(res, 404, {\n          error: \"not found\",\n          discovery: `${CONFIG.origin}/.well-known/agent-card.json`,\n        });\n      }\n    }\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(\"unhandled\", e);\n    return send(res, 500, { error: \"internal error\" });\n  }\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Boot: verify config against the chain before accepting traffic. A wrong USDC\n// domain or a registry address with no code produces failures that are very\n// hard to diagnose from the client side.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function verifyChainConfig() {\n  const chainId = await rpc.getChainId();\n  if (chainId !== CONFIG.chainId) {\n    throw new Error(`RPC is chain ${chainId}, expected ${CONFIG.chainId} (Base)`);\n  }\n\n  const erc20 = [\n    { name: \"name\", type: \"function\", stateMutability: \"view\", inputs: [], outputs: [{ type: \"string\" }] },\n    { name: \"version\", type: \"function\", stateMutability: \"view\", inputs: [], outputs: [{ type: \"string\" }] },\n    { name: \"decimals\", type: \"function\", stateMutability: \"view\", inputs: [], outputs: [{ type: \"uint8\" }] },\n  ] as const;\n\n  const [name, version, decimals] = await Promise.all([\n    rpc.readContract({ address: CONFIG.usdc, abi: erc20, functionName: \"name\" }),\n    rpc\n      .readContract({ address: CONFIG.usdc, abi: erc20, functionName: \"version\" })\n      .catch(() => usdcDomain.version),\n    rpc.readContract({ address: CONFIG.usdc, abi: erc20, functionName: \"decimals\" }),\n  ]);\n\n  if (Number(decimals) !== 6) {\n    throw new Error(`USDC decimals ${decimals} != 6; PRICE_ATOMIC would be wrong`);\n  }\n  usdcDomain = { name: String(name), version: String(version) };\n\n  for (const [label, addr] of [\n    [\"IdentityRegistry\", CONFIG.identityRegistry],\n    [\"ReputationRegistry\", CONFIG.reputationRegistry],\n  ] as const) {\n    const code = await rpc.getCode({ address: addr });\n    if (!code || code === \"0x\") console.warn(`WARN ${label} ${addr} has no code on chain ${chainId}`);\n  }\n\n  console.log(\n    `usdc=${CONFIG.usdc} domain=${usdcDomain.name}/v${usdcDomain.version} ` +\n      `price=${CONFIG.priceAtomic} operator=${operator.address}`,\n  );\n}\n\nverifyChainConfig()\n  .catch((e) => {\n    console.error(`chain config check failed: ${e.message}`);\n    if (process.env.ALLOW_UNVERIFIED_BOOT !== \"1\") process.exit(1);\n  })\n  .finally(() => {\n    server.listen(CONFIG.port, () => {\n      console.log(`forecast.example.com agentId=${CONFIG.agentId} on :${CONFIG.port}`);\n      console.log(`  card  ${CONFIG.origin}/.well-known/agent-card.json`);\n      console.log(`  paid  ${CONFIG.origin}/v1/forecast?lat=52.52&lon=13.40  ($0.35 USDC / Base)`);\n    });\n  });\n\nexport { server, CONFIG, agentCard, checkRequirements, issueFeedbackReceipt };\n\n// Silence unused-import lint if createHash is dropped from a future edit.\nvoid createHash;\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":15,"oldLines":7,"newStart":15,"newLines":7,"lines":["  */"," "," import { createServer, IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from \"node:http\";","-import { createHash, randomUUID } from \"node:crypto\";","+import { randomUUID } from \"node:crypto\";"," import {","   createPublicClient,","   http as viemHttp,"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"70c57b0d-8dbd-49ea-ac0e-5bfe1df64e0f","uuid":"9a5f903f-fc23-4767-b1d9-96be2872b5f4","timestamp":"2026-07-27T11:25:37.031Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-07-27T111530Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/server.ts","oldString":"export { server, CONFIG, agentCard, checkRequirements, issueFeedbackReceipt };\n\n// Silence unused-import lint if createHash is dropped from a future edit.\nvoid createHash;","newString":"export { server, CONFIG, agentCard, checkRequirements, issueFeedbackReceipt };","originalFile":"/**\n * forecast.example.com — service side.\n *\n * Sells point weather forecasts to autonomous agents for $0.35/call, settled in\n * USDC on Base. See design.md.\n *\n *   discovery : ERC-8004 Identity Registry (onchain) -> tokenURI -> agent card\n *   payment   : x402 (HTTP 402 + X-PAYMENT) over EIP-3009 transferWithAuthorization\n *   gasless   : the facilitator broadcasts and pays gas; the caller only signs\n *   ratings   : ERC-8004 Reputation Registry, gated by a receipt we sign per call\n *\n * No accounts, no API keys, no human in any step.\n *\n * Run: npx tsx server.ts     (Node >= 20 for global fetch)\n */\n\nimport { createServer, IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from \"node:http\";\nimport { randomUUID } from \"node:crypto\";\nimport {\n  createPublicClient,\n  http as viemHttp,\n  getAddress,\n  isAddress,\n  keccak256,\n  toHex,\n  type Address,\n  type Hex,\n} from \"viem\";\nimport { base } from \"viem/chains\";\nimport { privateKeyToAccount } from \"viem/accounts\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Config. Everything chain-shaped is config, not a literal buried in logic, and\n// is re-verified against the chain at boot (see verifyChainConfig).\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst env = (k: string, fallback?: string): string => {\n  const v = process.env[k] ?? fallback;\n  if (v === undefined) throw new Error(`missing required env ${k}`);\n  return v;\n};\n\nconst CONFIG = {\n  port: Number(env(\"PORT\", \"8080\")),\n  origin: env(\"ORIGIN\", \"https://forecast.example.com\"),\n\n  // --- payment ---\n  network: \"base\" as const,\n  chainId: 8453,\n  /** Native USDC on Base. Implements EIP-3009. Bridged USDbC does NOT — do not swap this. */\n  usdc: getAddress(env(\"USDC_ADDRESS\", \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\")),\n  /** $0.35 in USDC atomic units (6 decimals). */\n  priceAtomic: BigInt(env(\"PRICE_ATOMIC\", \"350000\")),\n  /** Where the USDC lands. Cold key, separate from the signer. */\n  treasury: getAddress(env(\"TREASURY_ADDRESS\", \"0x000000000000000000000000000000000000dEaD\")),\n  facilitatorUrl: env(\"FACILITATOR_URL\", \"https://x402.org/facilitator\").replace(/\\/$/, \"\"),\n  facilitatorAuth: process.env.FACILITATOR_API_KEY, // optional, provider-specific\n\n  // --- identity / reputation (ERC-8004) ---\n  agentId: BigInt(env(\"AGENT_ID\", \"4711\")),\n  identityRegistry: getAddress(\n    env(\"IDENTITY_REGISTRY\", \"0x000000000000000000000000000000000000dEaD\"),\n  ),\n  reputationRegistry: getAddress(\n    env(\"REPUTATION_REGISTRY\", \"0x000000000000000000000000000000000000dEaD\"),\n  ),\n  validationRegistry: process.env.VALIDATION_REGISTRY,\n\n  rpcUrl: env(\"BASE_RPC_URL\", \"https://mainnet.base.org\"),\n  /** Hot key in KMS in production. Signs receipts + response attestations. Holds no funds. */\n  operatorKey: env(\"AGENT_OPERATOR_KEY\", `0x${\"11\".repeat(32)}`) as Hex,\n\n  upstreamUrl: env(\"WEATHER_UPSTREAM\", \"https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast\"),\n\n  /** Reject payments whose authorization expires too soon to survive settlement. */\n  minValiditySecondsLeft: 30,\n  maxTimeoutSeconds: 60,\n  /** A receipt is usable for one rating, for this long. */\n  receiptTtlSeconds: 30 * 24 * 3600,\n} as const;\n\nconst operator = privateKeyToAccount(CONFIG.operatorKey);\nconst rpc = createPublicClient({ chain: base, transport: viemHttp(CONFIG.rpcUrl) });\n\n/**\n * USDC's EIP-712 domain, read from chain at boot. Clients need name+version to\n * produce a valid EIP-3009 signature; a hardcoded guess that drifts produces\n * signatures that fail onchain with no useful error message.\n */\nlet usdcDomain = { name: \"USD Coin\", version: \"2\" };\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// x402 types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface PaymentRequirements {\n  scheme: \"exact\";\n  network: string;\n  maxAmountRequired: string;\n  resource: string;\n  description: string;\n  mimeType: string;\n  payTo: Address;\n  maxTimeoutSeconds: number;\n  asset: Address;\n  outputSchema?: unknown;\n  extra: { name: string; version: string };\n}\n\ninterface ExactEvmAuthorization {\n  from: Address;\n  to: Address;\n  value: string;\n  validAfter: string;\n  validBefore: string;\n  nonce: Hex;\n}\n\ninterface PaymentPayload {\n  x402Version: number;\n  scheme: string;\n  network: string;\n  payload: { signature: Hex; authorization: ExactEvmAuthorization };\n}\n\ninterface SettleResult {\n  success: boolean;\n  errorReason?: string;\n  transaction?: Hex;\n  network?: string;\n  payer?: Address;\n}\n\nconst X402_VERSION = 1;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Facilitator. Single seam: swap this for a self-hosted relayer that broadcasts\n// transferWithAuthorization itself without touching anything else.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface Facilitator {\n  verify(\n    p: PaymentPayload,\n    r: PaymentRequirements,\n  ): Promise<{ isValid: boolean; invalidReason?: string; payer?: Address }>;\n  settle(p: PaymentPayload, r: PaymentRequirements): Promise<SettleResult>;\n}\n\nconst httpFacilitator: Facilitator = {\n  async verify(paymentPayload, paymentRequirements) {\n    return post(\"/verify\", { x402Version: X402_VERSION, paymentPayload, paymentRequirements });\n  },\n  async settle(paymentPayload, paymentRequirements) {\n    return post(\"/settle\", { x402Version: X402_VERSION, paymentPayload, paymentRequirements });\n  },\n};\n\nasync function post(path: string, body: unknown): Promise<any> {\n  const headers: Record<string, string> = { \"content-type\": \"application/json\" };\n  if (CONFIG.facilitatorAuth) headers.authorization = `Bearer ${CONFIG.facilitatorAuth}`;\n\n  const res = await fetch(CONFIG.facilitatorUrl + path, {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers,\n    body: JSON.stringify(body, jsonBigint),\n    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(CONFIG.maxTimeoutSeconds * 1000),\n  });\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    throw new Error(`facilitator ${path} -> ${res.status} ${await res.text().catch(() => \"\")}`);\n  }\n  return res.json();\n}\n\nconst jsonBigint = (_k: string, v: unknown) => (typeof v === \"bigint\" ? v.toString() : v);\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Idempotency. Keyed on the EIP-3009 nonce, which USDC itself consumes on\n// settle. Without this, a client whose connection drops after settlement has\n// paid and cannot retry: the replayed authorization now reverts onchain.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface Settled {\n  nonce: Hex;\n  payer: Address;\n  txHash?: Hex;\n  body: unknown;\n  receipt: unknown;\n  at: number;\n}\n\nconst settledCalls = new Map<Hex, Settled>();\nconst SETTLED_TTL_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;\n\nsetInterval(() => {\n  const cutoff = Date.now() - SETTLED_TTL_MS;\n  for (const [k, v] of settledCalls) if (v.at < cutoff) settledCalls.delete(k);\n}, 60_000).unref();\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Published documents\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst caip10 = (addr: Address) => `eip155:${CONFIG.chainId}:${addr}`;\n\nfunction paymentRequirements(resource: string, description: string): PaymentRequirements {\n  return {\n    scheme: \"exact\",\n    network: CONFIG.network,\n    maxAmountRequired: CONFIG.priceAtomic.toString(),\n    resource,\n    description,\n    mimeType: \"application/json\",\n    payTo: CONFIG.treasury,\n    maxTimeoutSeconds: CONFIG.maxTimeoutSeconds,\n    asset: CONFIG.usdc,\n    outputSchema: FORECAST_OUTPUT_SCHEMA,\n    extra: { name: usdcDomain.name, version: usdcDomain.version },\n  };\n}\n\nconst FORECAST_OUTPUT_SCHEMA = {\n  type: \"object\",\n  required: [\"location\", \"generatedAt\", \"hourly\"],\n  properties: {\n    location: { type: \"object\", properties: { lat: { type: \"number\" }, lon: { type: \"number\" } } },\n    generatedAt: { type: \"string\", format: \"date-time\" },\n    units: { type: \"object\" },\n    hourly: { type: \"array\", items: { type: \"object\" } },\n  },\n} as const;\n\n/**\n * A2A Agent Card extended with the ERC-8004 fields. Served at\n * /.well-known/agent-card.json, which is what the identity NFT's tokenURI\n * points at. `registrations[].signature` closes the loop offchain -> onchain so\n * a stranger can verify both directions before trusting anything here.\n */\nfunction agentCard() {\n  return {\n    protocolVersion: \"0.3.0\",\n    name: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description:\n      \"Point weather forecasts, 0-168h, global. $0.35 per call, USDC on Base, x402. No account required.\",\n    url: `${CONFIG.origin}/v1`,\n    version: \"1.2.0\",\n    capabilities: { streaming: false, pushNotifications: false, stateTransitionHistory: false },\n    defaultInputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    defaultOutputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n\n    skills: [\n      {\n        id: \"point-forecast\",\n        name: \"Point forecast\",\n        description: \"Hourly and daily forecast for a lat/lon, up to 168 hours ahead.\",\n        tags: [\"weather\", \"forecast\", \"x402\", \"usdc\"],\n        inputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n        outputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n        examples: [`GET ${CONFIG.origin}/v1/forecast?lat=52.52&lon=13.40&hours=48`],\n      },\n    ],\n\n    // ERC-8004: binds this document back to the onchain identity.\n    registrations: [\n      {\n        agentId: Number(CONFIG.agentId),\n        agentRegistry: caip10(CONFIG.identityRegistry),\n        signature: process.env.REGISTRATION_SIGNATURE ?? \"0x\",\n      },\n    ],\n    trustModels: [\"reputation\", \"crypto-economic\"],\n\n    // How to pay. The 402 challenge is authoritative; this is a hint for planners.\n    x402: {\n      x402Version: X402_VERSION,\n      endpoints: [\n        {\n          resource: `${CONFIG.origin}/v1/forecast`,\n          method: \"GET\",\n          ...paymentRequirements(`${CONFIG.origin}/v1/forecast`, \"Point weather forecast\"),\n        },\n      ],\n    },\n\n    // Where ratings go, and which key signs the receipts that authorize them.\n    feedback: {\n      registry: caip10(CONFIG.reputationRegistry),\n      authScheme: \"erc8004-feedback-auth-v1\",\n      signer: operator.address,\n      relay: `${CONFIG.origin}/v1/feedback/relay`,\n      supportedTags: [\"accuracy\", \"latency\", \"availability\"],\n    },\n    validation: CONFIG.validationRegistry\n      ? { registry: caip10(getAddress(CONFIG.validationRegistry)) }\n      : undefined,\n\n    openapi: `${CONFIG.origin}/openapi.json`,\n  };\n}\n\n/** x402 resource list — the machine-readable price sheet. */\nfunction x402Directory() {\n  return {\n    x402Version: X402_VERSION,\n    resources: [\n      {\n        resource: `${CONFIG.origin}/v1/forecast`,\n        method: \"GET\",\n        accepts: [paymentRequirements(`${CONFIG.origin}/v1/forecast`, \"Point weather forecast\")],\n        lastUpdated: new Date().toISOString(),\n      },\n    ],\n  };\n}\n\nfunction openapi() {\n  return {\n    openapi: \"3.1.0\",\n    info: {\n      title: \"forecast.example.com\",\n      version: \"1.2.0\",\n      description: \"Weather forecasts priced per call in USDC on Base via x402.\",\n    },\n    servers: [{ url: `${CONFIG.origin}/v1` }],\n    paths: {\n      \"/forecast\": {\n        get: {\n          summary: \"Point forecast\",\n          parameters: [\n            { name: \"lat\", in: \"query\", required: true, schema: { type: \"number\" } },\n            { name: \"lon\", in: \"query\", required: true, schema: { type: \"number\" } },\n            {\n              name: \"hours\",\n              in: \"query\",\n              schema: { type: \"integer\", minimum: 1, maximum: 168, default: 48 },\n            },\n            {\n              name: \"X-PAYMENT\",\n              in: \"header\",\n              required: true,\n              schema: { type: \"string\" },\n              description: \"base64 x402 payment payload (EIP-3009 authorization + signature)\",\n            },\n          ],\n          responses: {\n            \"200\": { description: \"Forecast + signed feedback receipt\" },\n            \"402\": { description: \"Payment required; body carries x402 `accepts`\" },\n            \"502\": { description: \"Upstream unavailable; no payment was settled\" },\n          },\n        },\n      },\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Payment validation\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction decodePaymentHeader(header: string): PaymentPayload {\n  const json = Buffer.from(header, \"base64\").toString(\"utf8\");\n  const parsed = JSON.parse(json) as PaymentPayload;\n  if (!parsed?.payload?.authorization || !parsed.payload.signature) {\n    throw new Error(\"malformed payment payload\");\n  }\n  return parsed;\n}\n\n/**\n * Local structural checks, run BEFORE the facilitator call. Never trust the\n * payload's own claims about what it is paying for: an attacker-supplied\n * payload should cost us a few microseconds, not a facilitator round trip.\n */\nfunction checkRequirements(p: PaymentPayload, req: PaymentRequirements): string | null {\n  const a = p.payload.authorization;\n  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);\n\n  if (p.x402Version !== X402_VERSION) return `unsupported x402Version ${p.x402Version}`;\n  if (p.scheme !== req.scheme) return `unsupported scheme ${p.scheme}`;\n  if (p.network !== req.network) return `wrong network ${p.network}, expected ${req.network}`;\n  if (!isAddress(a.from) || !isAddress(a.to)) return \"bad address in authorization\";\n  if (getAddress(a.to) !== req.payTo) return \"authorization pays the wrong recipient\";\n\n  let value: bigint;\n  try {\n    value = BigInt(a.value);\n  } catch {\n    return \"bad value\";\n  }\n  if (value < BigInt(req.maxAmountRequired)) {\n    return `underpaid: ${a.value} < ${req.maxAmountRequired}`;\n  }\n\n  if (!/^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/.test(a.nonce)) return \"nonce must be 32 bytes\";\n  if (Number(a.validAfter) > now) return \"authorization not yet valid\";\n  if (Number(a.validBefore) < now + CONFIG.minValiditySecondsLeft) {\n    return \"authorization expires too soon to settle\";\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Feedback receipt (ERC-8004). Signed only after settlement, before we know the\n// score, and usable against a registry we do not control — so we cannot\n// suppress a bad rating, and a forged one costs a real $0.35 from a real payer.\n//\n// NOTE: ERC-8004 is a draft. Pin this struct + domain to the ABI of the\n// registry you actually mint into before going live.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst FEEDBACK_AUTH_TYPES = {\n  FeedbackAuth: [\n    { name: \"agentId\", type: \"uint256\" },\n    { name: \"clientAddress\", type: \"address\" },\n    { name: \"indexLimit\", type: \"uint256\" },\n    { name: \"expiry\", type: \"uint256\" },\n    { name: \"chainId\", type: \"uint256\" },\n    { name: \"identityRegistry\", type: \"address\" },\n    { name: \"signerAddress\", type: \"address\" },\n  ],\n} as const;\n\nasync function issueFeedbackReceipt(payer: Address, settlementTx?: Hex) {\n  const domain = {\n    name: \"ERC8004ReputationRegistry\",\n    version: \"1\",\n    chainId: CONFIG.chainId,\n    verifyingContract: CONFIG.reputationRegistry,\n  } as const;\n\n  const message = {\n    agentId: CONFIG.agentId,\n    clientAddress: payer,\n    indexLimit: 1n, // one rating per paid call\n    expiry: BigInt(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + CONFIG.receiptTtlSeconds),\n    chainId: BigInt(CONFIG.chainId),\n    identityRegistry: CONFIG.identityRegistry,\n    signerAddress: operator.address,\n  } as const;\n\n  const signature = await operator.signTypedData({\n    domain,\n    types: FEEDBACK_AUTH_TYPES,\n    primaryType: \"FeedbackAuth\",\n    message,\n  });\n\n  return {\n    scheme: \"erc8004-feedback-auth-v1\",\n    domain,\n    primaryType: \"FeedbackAuth\",\n    message: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(message, jsonBigint)),\n    signature,\n    settlementTx,\n    howTo: {\n      contract: caip10(CONFIG.reputationRegistry),\n      method:\n        \"giveFeedback(uint256 agentId, uint8 score, bytes32 tag1, bytes32 tag2, string fileuri, bytes32 filehash, bytes feedbackAuth)\",\n      scoreRange: [0, 100],\n      tags: [\"accuracy\", \"latency\", \"availability\"],\n      gaslessRelay: `${CONFIG.origin}/v1/feedback/relay`,\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n/** Attributable response: lets a client cite a bad answer as evidence later. */\nasync function attestResponse(requestHash: Hex, body: unknown): Promise<Hex> {\n  const responseHash = keccak256(toHex(JSON.stringify(body)));\n  return operator.signMessage({\n    message: { raw: keccak256(toHex(`${CONFIG.agentId}:${requestHash}:${responseHash}`)) },\n  });\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// The product\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface ForecastQuery {\n  lat: number;\n  lon: number;\n  hours: number;\n  units: \"metric\" | \"imperial\";\n}\n\nfunction parseForecastQuery(url: URL): ForecastQuery | { error: string } {\n  const lat = Number(url.searchParams.get(\"lat\"));\n  const lon = Number(url.searchParams.get(\"lon\"));\n  const hours = Number(url.searchParams.get(\"hours\") ?? \"48\");\n  const units = (url.searchParams.get(\"units\") ?? \"metric\") as ForecastQuery[\"units\"];\n\n  if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || lat < -90 || lat > 90) return { error: \"lat must be -90..90\" };\n  if (!Number.isFinite(lon) || lon < -180 || lon > 180) return { error: \"lon must be -180..180\" };\n  if (!Number.isInteger(hours) || hours < 1 || hours > 168) return { error: \"hours must be 1..168\" };\n  if (units !== \"metric\" && units !== \"imperial\") return { error: \"units must be metric|imperial\" };\n  return { lat, lon, hours, units };\n}\n\nasync function fetchForecast(q: ForecastQuery) {\n  const u = new URL(CONFIG.upstreamUrl);\n  u.searchParams.set(\"latitude\", String(q.lat));\n  u.searchParams.set(\"longitude\", String(q.lon));\n  u.searchParams.set(\"hourly\", \"temperature_2m,precipitation,wind_speed_10m,weather_code\");\n  u.searchParams.set(\"forecast_hours\", String(q.hours));\n  if (q.units === \"imperial\") {\n    u.searchParams.set(\"temperature_unit\", \"fahrenheit\");\n    u.searchParams.set(\"wind_speed_unit\", \"mph\");\n  }\n\n  const res = await fetch(u, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000) });\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`upstream ${res.status}`);\n  const raw = (await res.json()) as any;\n\n  const times: string[] = raw.hourly?.time ?? [];\n  return {\n    location: { lat: q.lat, lon: q.lon },\n    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    units: {\n      temperature: q.units === \"imperial\" ? \"degF\" : \"degC\",\n      windSpeed: q.units === \"imperial\" ? \"mph\" : \"km/h\",\n      precipitation: \"mm\",\n    },\n    hourly: times.slice(0, q.hours).map((t, i) => ({\n      time: t,\n      temperature: raw.hourly.temperature_2m?.[i] ?? null,\n      precipitation: raw.hourly.precipitation?.[i] ?? null,\n      windSpeed: raw.hourly.wind_speed_10m?.[i] ?? null,\n      weatherCode: raw.hourly.weather_code?.[i] ?? null,\n    })),\n    source: \"open-meteo\",\n  };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Handlers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction send(res: ServerResponse, status: number, body: unknown, headers: Record<string, string> = {}) {\n  const payload = typeof body === \"string\" ? body : JSON.stringify(body, jsonBigint, 2);\n  res.writeHead(status, {\n    \"content-type\": \"application/json; charset=utf-8\",\n    \"access-control-allow-origin\": \"*\",\n    \"access-control-expose-headers\": \"x-payment-response, x-agent-signature\",\n    ...headers,\n  });\n  res.end(payload);\n}\n\nfunction challenge(res: ServerResponse, resource: string, error: string, status = 402) {\n  send(res, status, {\n    x402Version: X402_VERSION,\n    error,\n    accepts: [paymentRequirements(resource, \"Point weather forecast, 0-168h\")],\n  });\n}\n\n/** The paid endpoint. Ordering — verify, work, settle, respond — is deliberate. */\nasync function handleForecast(req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse, url: URL) {\n  const resource = `${CONFIG.origin}${url.pathname}${url.search}`;\n\n  const q = parseForecastQuery(url);\n  if (\"error\" in q) return send(res, 400, { error: q.error });\n\n  const header = req.headers[\"x-payment\"];\n  if (!header || typeof header !== \"string\") {\n    return challenge(res, resource, \"X-PAYMENT header is required\");\n  }\n\n  let payment: PaymentPayload;\n  try {\n    payment = decodePaymentHeader(header);\n  } catch (e) {\n    return challenge(res, resource, `invalid X-PAYMENT header: ${(e as Error).message}`);\n  }\n\n  const nonce = payment.payload.authorization.nonce;\n\n  // Idempotent retry of an already-settled call: they paid, give them the data.\n  const prior = settledCalls.get(nonce);\n  if (prior) {\n    return send(res, 200, { ...(prior.body as object), receipt: prior.receipt, replayed: true }, {\n      \"x-payment-response\": encodeSettleHeader({\n        success: true,\n        transaction: prior.txHash,\n        network: CONFIG.network,\n        payer: prior.payer,\n      }),\n    });\n  }\n\n  const requirements = paymentRequirements(resource, \"Point weather forecast, 0-168h\");\n\n  // 1. Local checks, then facilitator verification. No work done yet.\n  const structural = checkRequirements(payment, requirements);\n  if (structural) return challenge(res, resource, structural);\n\n  let verification: { isValid: boolean; invalidReason?: string; payer?: Address };\n  try {\n    verification = await httpFacilitator.verify(payment, requirements);\n  } catch (e) {\n    return send(res, 503, { error: `payment verification unavailable: ${(e as Error).message}` });\n  }\n  if (!verification.isValid) {\n    return challenge(res, resource, verification.invalidReason ?? \"payment verification failed\");\n  }\n\n  // 2. Do the work before settling. If the upstream is down the caller is never\n  //    charged: the authorization simply expires unused. We hold no balances,\n  //    so there is nothing to refund.\n  let forecast: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof fetchForecast>>;\n  try {\n    forecast = await fetchForecast(q);\n  } catch (e) {\n    return send(res, 502, {\n      error: \"upstream forecast source unavailable\",\n      detail: (e as Error).message,\n      charged: false,\n    });\n  }\n\n  // 3. Settle before responding, so data never leaves for a payment that failed\n  //    to land onchain. The facilitator submits transferWithAuthorization and\n  //    pays the gas — this is why the caller needs no ETH.\n  let settlement: SettleResult;\n  try {\n    settlement = await httpFacilitator.settle(payment, requirements);\n  } catch (e) {\n    return challenge(res, resource, `settlement failed: ${(e as Error).message}`);\n  }\n  if (!settlement.success) {\n    return challenge(res, resource, settlement.errorReason ?? \"settlement failed\");\n  }\n\n  // 4. Respond with the data, a signed attestation, and a receipt that lets the\n  //    caller rate us onchain without asking us for anything further.\n  const payer = getAddress(settlement.payer ?? verification.payer ?? payment.payload.authorization.from);\n  const requestHash = keccak256(toHex(resource));\n  const receipt = await issueFeedbackReceipt(payer, settlement.transaction);\n  const body = { ...forecast, requestId: randomUUID(), agentId: Number(CONFIG.agentId) };\n\n  settledCalls.set(nonce, {\n    nonce,\n    payer,\n    txHash: settlement.transaction,\n    body,\n    receipt,\n    at: Date.now(),\n  });\n\n  send(res, 200, { ...body, receipt }, {\n    \"x-payment-response\": encodeSettleHeader(settlement),\n    \"x-agent-signature\": await attestResponse(requestHash, body),\n  });\n}\n\nconst encodeSettleHeader = (s: SettleResult) =>\n  Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(s, jsonBigint)).toString(\"base64\");\n\n/**\n * Gasless rating relay for callers holding USDC but no ETH: they sign the\n * feedback intent, we pay the gas to submit it. The score lives inside their\n * signature, so we cannot alter it — and withholding negative ones is visible,\n * since settlement txs and feedback events are both public.\n */\nasync function handleFeedbackRelay(req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse) {\n  let intent: any;\n  try {\n    intent = JSON.parse(await readBody(req));\n  } catch {\n    return send(res, 400, { error: \"body must be JSON\" });\n  }\n\n  const required = [\"score\", \"signature\", \"feedbackAuth\", \"clientAddress\"];\n  const missing = required.filter((f) => intent[f] === undefined);\n  if (missing.length) return send(res, 400, { error: `missing fields: ${missing.join(\", \")}` });\n  if (!Number.isInteger(intent.score) || intent.score < 0 || intent.score > 100) {\n    return send(res, 400, { error: \"score must be an integer 0..100\" });\n  }\n\n  // Submitting requires a gas-funded relayer wallet + nonce management; wire the\n  // walletClient here once the registry's signed-intent entrypoint is pinned.\n  // Until then, tell the caller exactly how to submit it themselves.\n  return send(res, 501, {\n    error: \"relay not enabled on this deployment\",\n    submitYourself: {\n      contract: caip10(CONFIG.reputationRegistry),\n      method: \"giveFeedback\",\n      note: \"Or use any public ERC-4337 paymaster that accepts USDC for gas.\",\n    },\n  });\n}\n\nasync function handleHealth(res: ServerResponse) {\n  const [upstream, facilitator, chain] = await Promise.all([\n    probe(`${CONFIG.upstreamUrl}?latitude=0&longitude=0&hourly=temperature_2m&forecast_hours=1`),\n    probe(`${CONFIG.facilitatorUrl}/supported`),\n    rpc.getBlockNumber().then(\n      (n) => ({ ok: true, detail: `block ${n}` }),\n      (e) => ({ ok: false, detail: (e as Error).message }),\n    ),\n  ]);\n  const ok = upstream.ok && chain.ok;\n  send(res, ok ? 200 : 503, {\n    ok,\n    agentId: Number(CONFIG.agentId),\n    checks: { upstream, facilitator, chain },\n  });\n}\n\nasync function probe(url: string) {\n  try {\n    const r = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(4000) });\n    return { ok: r.ok, detail: `HTTP ${r.status}` };\n  } catch (e) {\n    return { ok: false, detail: (e as Error).message };\n  }\n}\n\nfunction readBody(req: IncomingMessage): Promise<string> {\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    let data = \"\";\n    req.on(\"data\", (c) => {\n      data += c;\n      if (data.length > 64_000) reject(new Error(\"body too large\"));\n    });\n    req.on(\"end\", () => resolve(data));\n    req.on(\"error\", reject);\n  });\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Router\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst server = createServer(async (req, res) => {\n  const url = new URL(req.url ?? \"/\", CONFIG.origin);\n\n  if (req.method === \"OPTIONS\") {\n    res.writeHead(204, {\n      \"access-control-allow-origin\": \"*\",\n      \"access-control-allow-headers\": \"content-type, x-payment\",\n      \"access-control-allow-methods\": \"GET, POST, OPTIONS\",\n    });\n    return res.end();\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const route = `${req.method} ${url.pathname}`;\n    switch (route) {\n      // --- discovery: free, static, cacheable, no payment ---\n      case \"GET /.well-known/agent-card.json\":\n        return send(res, 200, agentCard(), { \"cache-control\": \"public, max-age=300\" });\n      case \"GET /.well-known/x402\":\n        return send(res, 200, x402Directory(), { \"cache-control\": \"public, max-age=300\" });\n      case \"GET /openapi.json\":\n        return send(res, 200, openapi(), { \"cache-control\": \"public, max-age=300\" });\n\n      // --- the 402 challenge on its own, for planning ---\n      case \"GET /v1/quote\":\n        return challenge(\n          res,\n          `${CONFIG.origin}/v1/forecast${url.search}`,\n          \"quote only; resend to /v1/forecast with X-PAYMENT\",\n          200,\n        );\n\n      // --- the product ---\n      case \"GET /v1/forecast\":\n        return await handleForecast(req, res, url);\n\n      // --- ratings ---\n      case \"POST /v1/feedback/relay\":\n        return await handleFeedbackRelay(req, res);\n\n      case \"GET /healthz\":\n        return await handleHealth(res);\n\n      default: {\n        if (req.method === \"GET\" && url.pathname.startsWith(\"/v1/receipt/\")) {\n          const nonce = url.pathname.slice(\"/v1/receipt/\".length) as Hex;\n          const prior = settledCalls.get(nonce);\n          if (!prior) return send(res, 404, { error: \"unknown or expired settlement nonce\" });\n          return send(res, 200, { receipt: prior.receipt, settlementTx: prior.txHash });\n        }\n        return send(res, 404, {\n          error: \"not found\",\n          discovery: `${CONFIG.origin}/.well-known/agent-card.json`,\n        });\n      }\n    }\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(\"unhandled\", e);\n    return send(res, 500, { error: \"internal error\" });\n  }\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Boot: verify config against the chain before accepting traffic. A wrong USDC\n// domain or a registry address with no code produces failures that are very\n// hard to diagnose from the client side.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function verifyChainConfig() {\n  const chainId = await rpc.getChainId();\n  if (chainId !== CONFIG.chainId) {\n    throw new Error(`RPC is chain ${chainId}, expected ${CONFIG.chainId} (Base)`);\n  }\n\n  const erc20 = [\n    { name: \"name\", type: \"function\", stateMutability: \"view\", inputs: [], outputs: [{ type: \"string\" }] },\n    { name: \"version\", type: \"function\", stateMutability: \"view\", inputs: [], outputs: [{ type: \"string\" }] },\n    { name: \"decimals\", type: \"function\", stateMutability: \"view\", inputs: [], outputs: [{ type: \"uint8\" }] },\n  ] as const;\n\n  const [name, version, decimals] = await Promise.all([\n    rpc.readContract({ address: CONFIG.usdc, abi: erc20, functionName: \"name\" }),\n    rpc\n      .readContract({ address: CONFIG.usdc, abi: erc20, functionName: \"version\" })\n      .catch(() => usdcDomain.version),\n    rpc.readContract({ address: CONFIG.usdc, abi: erc20, functionName: \"decimals\" }),\n  ]);\n\n  if (Number(decimals) !== 6) {\n    throw new Error(`USDC decimals ${decimals} != 6; PRICE_ATOMIC would be wrong`);\n  }\n  usdcDomain = { name: String(name), version: String(version) };\n\n  for (const [label, addr] of [\n    [\"IdentityRegistry\", CONFIG.identityRegistry],\n    [\"ReputationRegistry\", CONFIG.reputationRegistry],\n  ] as const) {\n    const code = await rpc.getCode({ address: addr });\n    if (!code || code === \"0x\") console.warn(`WARN ${label} ${addr} has no code on chain ${chainId}`);\n  }\n\n  console.log(\n    `usdc=${CONFIG.usdc} domain=${usdcDomain.name}/v${usdcDomain.version} ` +\n      `price=${CONFIG.priceAtomic} operator=${operator.address}`,\n  );\n}\n\nverifyChainConfig()\n  .catch((e) => {\n    console.error(`chain config check failed: ${e.message}`);\n    if (process.env.ALLOW_UNVERIFIED_BOOT !== \"1\") process.exit(1);\n  })\n  .finally(() => {\n    server.listen(CONFIG.port, () => {\n      console.log(`forecast.example.com agentId=${CONFIG.agentId} on :${CONFIG.port}`);\n      console.log(`  card  ${CONFIG.origin}/.well-known/agent-card.json`);\n      console.log(`  paid  ${CONFIG.origin}/v1/forecast?lat=52.52&lon=13.40  ($0.35 USDC / Base)`);\n    });\n  });\n\nexport { server, CONFIG, agentCard, checkRequirements, issueFeedbackReceipt };\n\n// Silence unused-import lint if createHash is dropped from a future edit.\nvoid createHash;\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":851,"oldLines":6,"newStart":851,"newLines":3,"lines":["   });"," "," export { server, CONFIG, agentCard, checkRequirements, issueFeedbackReceipt };","-","-// Silence unused-import lint if createHash is dropped from a future edit.","-void createHash;"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}
